# State, command and event matrix (B06, B07, B08, B09 and B10 served)

Generated by `tools/matrix.mjs` — do not edit; `pnpm run matrix` (part of `pnpm run verify`) fails when this file is not the
generated one or when a source it is generated from changed without it. Sources: the registry's `state_transitions.json`,
`commands.json` and `cases.json` (provenance-verified, `@kauro/contracts`), the platform machines of `@kauro/domain` (EndClient
and Project; UploadSession, StoredObject, SourceFile and RetentionRecord of the source lifecycle, B07; the commerce machines of B09;
SupportCase of B11) and its event registry
(`EVENT_TYPES`, `EVENT_SUBSCRIPTIONS`), the served command specs and their outcome catalogues of `@kauro/application`
(`B06_COMMANDS`, `B07_COMMANDS` and, since mandate B08-COMMANDS-EXECUTION, `EXECUTION_COMMANDS` and, since mandate B08-COMMANDS-AUDIT, `AUDIT_COMMANDS`, and B11's `SUPPORT_COMMANDS`; `COMMAND_OUTCOMES`, enforced by the api's send guard), the reads and the document
intakes with theirs (`READ_ROUTES`, `READ_OUTCOMES`, `INTAKE_ROUTES`, `INTAKE_OUTCOMES`; B07's served by mandate B07-COMMANDS, B08's decision artifact intake by B08-COMMANDS-AUDIT), the transition
seeds of the migrations (`kauro.state_transitions`), the events each handler publishes (read from its source) and the
owner table and applicability record of `tools/matrix.mjs`, whose every citation the check resolves. Package B09's served
commands and reads join the served sections from `COMMERCE_SERVED_COMMAND_IDS` and `COMMERCE_SERVED_READ_IDS` (decision
D-REQ-B7): their specs, catalogues (`COMMERCE_COMMAND_OUTCOMES`, `COMMERCE_READ_OUTCOMES`) and published events
(`COMMERCE_COMMAND_EVENTS`, which the route tests prove complete). Package B10's served commands join them from
`COMMERCIAL_SERVED_COMMAND_IDS` (specs `COMMERCIAL_COMMANDS`, catalogues `COMMERCIAL_COMMAND_OUTCOMES`, events `COMMERCIAL_COMMAND_EVENTS`). Authority:
prompts/B06 "Provide a complete state/command/event matrix"; 10 "Contract tests and compatibility"; 07 "Acceptance
obligations". Re-check finding N13.

Registry: commands.json `6fa1a124821609a5ac911779304e8876df1a6954c530e895604da107826af0b6`, state_transitions.json `5d770459eb0ce40148edcc31cd01ed72f07fcd1666853c2d8b3240b11d94a6f5`.

## 1. Every edge of every machine

`Performed by` names the registry command (and the package its cases belong to) or, where no registry command names the
edge, the owning package and why. `Stored` says what the platform holds today: **route** — a served command performs it;
**database** — the schema (migrations 0001–0007 of B06, 0008 the shared aggregate-kind registry, 0009–0012 of B07, 0013–0017 of B09, 0018 of their merge, 0019 of B09's package-close review, 0020 of B07's, 0021–0024 of B08, 0025–0027 of B11) stores the aggregate and seeds the edge in
`kauro.state_transitions` (every other edge is refused, SQLSTATE KA008), so the owning package's writer is admitted and
checked; **—** — the aggregate is not stored yet.

### UploadSession

platform machine of the source lifecycle (B07 decision D-UP-1); initial RESERVED; terminal CANCELLED, EXPIRED, REJECTED; unlisted edges: REJECT_UNLESS_A_NEW_VERSIONED_CONTRACT_IS_ACCEPTED.

| RESERVED | session_issued | UPLOADING | CMD013 ReserveUpload (B07); the completion unit of work after the provider call, by the attempt holding the issuance fence (D-RUN-2) | upload_session.session_issued.v1 | route |
| UPLOADING | completion_reported | VERIFYING | CMD014 CompleteUpload (B07) | upload_session.completion_reported.v1 | route |
| VERIFYING | completion_not_observed | UPLOADING | B07: the UPLOAD_INSPECTION consumer (control plane) observes no complete object for the claim; the claim is cleared and the session stays usable until the deadline | upload_session.completion_not_observed.v1 | database |
| VERIFYING | inspection_admitted | ADMITTED | B07: the UPLOAD_INSPECTION consumer (control plane): observation, inspection, scan coverage, promotion and a receipt of the current attempt (S053, S054) | upload_session.admitted.v1 | database |
| VERIFYING | inspection_rejected | REJECTED | B07: the UPLOAD_INSPECTION consumer (control plane) records a rejecting receipt of the current attempt (D-INSP-3) | upload_session.rejected.v1 | database |
| RESERVED | cancel_upload | CANCELLED | CMD015 CancelUpload (B07); also CMD012 when it cancels a mutable predecessor's uploads | upload_session.cancelled.v1 | route |
| UPLOADING | cancel_upload | CANCELLED | CMD015 CancelUpload (B07); also CMD012 when it cancels a mutable predecessor's uploads | upload_session.cancelled.v1 | route |
| VERIFYING | cancel_upload | CANCELLED | CMD015 CancelUpload (B07); also CMD012 when it cancels a mutable predecessor's uploads | upload_session.cancelled.v1 | route |
| RESERVED | reservation_expired | EXPIRED | B07: the upload-deadline sweeper (control plane) at the application deadline, never earlier (R2-051) | upload_session.expired.v1 | database |
| UPLOADING | reservation_expired | EXPIRED | B07: the upload-deadline sweeper (control plane) at the application deadline, never earlier (R2-051) | upload_session.expired.v1 | database |
| VERIFYING | reservation_expired | EXPIRED | B07: the upload-deadline sweeper (control plane) at the application deadline, never earlier (R2-051) | upload_session.expired.v1 | database |
| ADMITTED | intake_deadline_reached | EXPIRED | B07: the upload-deadline sweeper (control plane): an admitted file no sealed revision retains leaves with its intake window (D-RET-1) | upload_session.expired.v1 | database |

platform machine of the source lifecycle (B07 decision D-OBJ-1); initial INTENDED; terminal DELETED; unlisted edges: REJECT_UNLESS_A_NEW_VERSIONED_CONTRACT_IS_ACCEPTED.

platform machine of the source lifecycle (B07 decision D-RET-2); initial ACTIVE; terminal DELETED, DELETED_WITH_RESIDUALS; unlisted edges: REJECT_UNLESS_A_NEW_VERSIONED_CONTRACT_IS_ACCEPTED.

| DELETING | cleanup_verified_with_residuals | DELETED_WITH_RESIDUALS | B16: verified managed deletion with recorded provider residuals | — (no event type registered: the owning package registers it with the edge) | database |

Approval has no registry machine: CMD025 ApprovePlan creates it (`approval.created.v1`); CMD026 WithdrawApproval and a
CMD023 RevisePlan of an APPROVED plan withdraw it once (`approval.withdrawn.v1`); the database keeps a live approval and an
APPROVED plan bound to each other both ways at COMMIT (SQLSTATE KA020, re-check finding N5).

## 2. The served commands

A B09 provider command's events are those of its whole run: the request handler's intent and claim and the payment service's
settle step that its call causes (D-PROTO-B5); `Step-up` of CMD032 is a fresh step-up unless a standing policy covers the refund.

| Command | Route | Role | Scope | Step-up | Edges | Events published | Outbox consumers |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CMD001 CreateAgency | POST /api/v1/agencies | VERIFIED_USER | NONE | — | Agency (initial DRAFT) | agency.created.v1, membership.created.v1 | agency.created.v1 → NOTIFICATIONS |
| CMD003 InviteMember | POST /api/v1/agencies/{agencyId}/invitations | AGENCY_OWNER_OR_ADMIN | AGENCY_PATH | — | Membership (initial INVITED) | membership.invited.v1 | membership.invited.v1 → NOTIFICATIONS |
| CMD004 AcceptInvitation | POST /api/v1/invitations/{invitationId}/accept | VERIFIED_USER | NONE | — | Membership intended_identity_accepts | membership.accepted.v1 | — |
| CMD005 ChangeMembership | POST /api/v1/memberships/{membershipId}/capabilities | AGENCY_OWNER | AGENCY_HEADER | fresh |  |  | — |
| CMD006 RevokeMembership | POST /api/v1/memberships/{membershipId}/revoke | AGENCY_OWNER_OR_ADMIN | AGENCY_HEADER | — | Membership inviter_revokes; Membership authorized_revocation | membership.revoked.v1 | — |
| CMD007 TransferOwnership | POST /api/v1/agencies/{agencyId}/ownership-transfers | AGENCY_OWNER | AGENCY_PATH | fresh |  |  | — |

## 2a. The source-lifecycle routes (B07)

Specified by `docs/design/B07-upload-source-lifecycle.md` and served by mandate B07-COMMANDS: the six commands are rows of section 2
with their catalogues in section 3 and their applicability in section 4; the reads are rows of section 5; the selection manifest
intake (D-SEL-2: content-idempotent, no journal entry) is section 5a.

| Route | Operation | Role | Success view | Authority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| POST /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId}/selection-manifests | SubmitSelectionManifest | AGENCY_OPERATOR | 201 SelectionManifestIntakeView | 10 "Upload and target routes" (selection edits require the revision version: the version is compared by CMD016); D-SEL-2 |
| GET /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId}/selection-manifests/{selectionManifestId} | GetSelectionManifest | AGENCY_READER | 200 SelectionManifestView | D-SEL-1 |
| GET /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId} | GetRevision | AGENCY_READER | 200 SourceRevisionView | 10 "Client and project routes"; 09 "Notifications and customer action" (dataAvailableUntil) |
| GET /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId}/inspection | GetInspection | AGENCY_READER | 200 InspectionView | 10 "Upload and target routes" (files, detected worksheets, parser choices and structured problems) |
| GET /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId}/uploads | ListUploads | AGENCY_READER | 200 UploadPage | 10 "Upload and target routes" (safe progress); 10 "Pagination, search and exports" |
| GET /api/v1/uploads/{uploadId} | GetUpload | AGENCY_READER | 200 UploadView | 10 "Upload and target routes" (GET returns safe progress without exposing the session URI to unrelated readers) |
| GET /api/v1/uploads/{uploadId}/session | GetUploadSession | AGENCY_OPERATOR | 200 ProtectedSessionView | 10 "Upload and target routes" (the provider session through the protected response only); 09 "Resumable session security"; D-SESSION-2 |
| GET /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId}/tables/{tableId}/preview | PreviewTable | AGENCY_READER | 200 TablePreviewView | 10 "Upload and target routes" (a bounded authorized sample with safe display escaping and no shared caching); D-SAPI-7 |

## 3. Error catalogue

Every problem a served command may answer (`COMMAND_OUTCOMES`). The api's send guard refuses any other problem on the route
(`422 INTEGRITY_FAILURE RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION`, handed to the internal-error sink), and `apps/api/openapi.json`
documents the same entries per status (`x-kauro-outcomes`). `current`: the problem carries the current safe version and
state — always, sometimes, or never (—). Frontends branch on code and reason, never on the title.

### CMD001 CreateAgency

| Status | Code | Reason | Field codes | current | Layer | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | HEADER_REPEATED | — | — | TRANSPORT | a single-valued envelope header was sent more than once; nothing that depends on it was decided (R41) |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | REQUEST_MALFORMED | — | — | TRANSPORT | the request's framing cannot be trusted (a body shorter or longer than its Content-Length) or it ended before it was complete (R39, R40) |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | CONTENT_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED | — | — | ADMISSION | the body is not application/json (UTF-8) |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | BODY_TOO_LARGE | — | — | ADMISSION | the body exceeds the policy's api.jsonBytes (1 MiB) |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_SYNTAX | — | — | ADMISSION | the body is not strict UTF-8 JSON |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_DUPLICATE_KEY | — | — | ADMISSION | an object of the body repeats a member name |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_DEPTH_EXCEEDED | — | — | ADMISSION | the body nests deeper than the parser bound |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_STRING_TOO_LONG | — | — | ADMISSION | a string of the body exceeds the parser bound |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | SCHEMA_VIOLATION | REQUIRED, UNKNOWN_FIELD, TYPE, FORMAT, RANGE, ENUM | — | ADMISSION | the body does not satisfy the closed request schema (R2-058, R2-059); each field error names the member and why |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MISSING | — | — | ADMISSION | the Idempotency-Key header is absent |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID | — | — | ADMISSION | the Idempotency-Key header is not a key of the accepted shape |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | AMBIGUOUS_CREDENTIALS | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | a session cookie beside an Authorization header, or two session cookies: nothing is decided for either (B11 D-AUTH-5); the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | — | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | no authenticated subject: the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | SUBJECT_UNAUTHENTICATED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the subject is not the identity the unit of work is bound to |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_DISABLED | — | — | ACCESS | the token's or the stored identity state is DISABLED |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED | — | — | ACCESS | the launch policy needs a verified email and enrolled MFA |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ORIGIN_REJECTED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request's Origin is not exactly an application origin (or absent), or the browser reports a cross-site request: nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | CSRF_TOKEN_INVALID | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request does not carry the session's CSRF token (X-Kauro-CSRF): nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
| 409 | IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT | REQUEST_DIGEST_DIFFERS | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the key was used with another request: nothing was executed again |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | OPERATION_PENDING | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the first request under this key is still in flight; retry with the same key |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 429 | ADMISSION_LIMITED | CREATION_RATE_LIMITED | — | — | COMMAND | the creation limit of the identity or of the client network is reached (08 Abuse controls, R34) |
| 429 | ADMISSION_LIMITED | DRAFT_AGENCY_EXISTS | — | — | COMMAND | the identity already created an agency that is still DRAFT (D-CMD-1) |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |

| 404 | NOT_FOUND | NOT_A_MEMBER | — | — | ACCESS | no live membership of the caller in the selected agency: a foreign and a nonexistent agency answer alike |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | — | — | — | COMMAND | the membership is not visible to the caller's agency |
| 409 | IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT | REQUEST_DIGEST_DIFFERS | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the key was used with another request: nothing was executed again |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | OPERATION_PENDING | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the first request under this key is still in flight; retry with the same key |
| 409 | STALE_VERSION | — | — | always | COMMAND | expectedVersion is not the membership's current version; the current safe version and state come back |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | MEMBERSHIP_NOT_ACTIVE | — | — | COMMAND | the target is not a live accepted membership (an INVITED one is reissued instead) |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | NO_SEMANTIC_CHANGE | — | always | COMMAND | the membership already has the requested role |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | LAST_OWNER_PROTECTED | — | always | COMMAND | the change would leave the agency without an active owner |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |

| 409 | STALE_VERSION | HASH_MISMATCH | — | always | COMMAND | selectionHash is not the hash of the selection the revision binds; current.mismatch names it |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | REVISION_SEALED | — | always | COMMAND | the revision is already sealed; current carries its version |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | REVISION_NOT_READY_TO_SEAL | — | always | COMMAND | the revision's selected scope is not evaluated and admitted (not READY_TO_SEAL) |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | UPLOADS_UNRESOLVED | — | always | COMMAND | a reservation, session or verification of the revision is still open: selection and seal need a settled inventory (D-SEL-3) |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | INPUT_NOT_ADMITTED | — | always | COMMAND | an included file's admission was revoked (its intake expired or its generation was lost): no executable sealed revision is left |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | GENERATION_CHANGED | — | always | COMMAND | an included file's accepted generation, digest or length is not the one the evaluation counted — as recorded, or as the provider holds it when the seal observes it (B07-R1) |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | REUSE_NOT_AVAILABLE | — | always | COMMAND | a reused file's bytes were first sealed fourteen days ago or more: their hard cap is behind this seal, so reuse needs a new upload (D-RET-3) |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | SOURCE_SERVICES_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | COMMAND | no object-storage adapter is bound in this edition (until the integration package binds one, D-SAPI-6); nothing was reserved |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | GENERATION_NOT_OBSERVED | — | — | COMMAND | the provider's current generation of an included file could not be observed at seal time; nothing was sealed, and a retry with the same key seals |

| 404 | NOT_FOUND | — | — | — | COMMAND | the project or the authorization is not visible to the caller's agency |
| 409 | IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT | REQUEST_DIGEST_DIFFERS | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the key was used with another request: nothing was executed again |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | OPERATION_PENDING | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the first request under this key is still in flight; retry with the same key |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | AGENCY_NOT_ACTIVE | — | always | COMMAND | customer data is processed only in an ACTIVE agency (07: DRAFT permits identity and agreement setup, not customer-data execution; B06 D-CMD-2) |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | PROJECT_NOT_OPEN | — | always | COMMAND | the project is not OPEN (closed or being deleted): it starts no execution |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | AUTHORIZATION_REVOKED | — | always | COMMAND | the commercial authorization is no longer ACTIVE (revoked before admission is PRE_EXECUTION): no job is bound to it (D8-AUTH-1); current names its version and state |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | PLAN_NOT_APPROVED | — | always | COMMAND | the authorization's approval is no longer live on an APPROVED plan (withdrawn or superseded) |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 422 | SOURCE_EXPIRED | — | — | — | COMMAND | the authorization's source revision is no longer SEALED and available (its retention deadline was reached, D8-STALE-1) |
| 422 | TARGET_STALE | — | — | — | COMMAND | the authorization's target contract is not confirmed and current (D8-STALE-1) |
| 422 | CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE | MODE_NOT_OFFERED | — | — | COMMAND | the authorization's mode is no longer offered (09: the mode capability is checked before work is queued) |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | EXECUTION_QUEUE_FULL | — | — | COMMAND | the agency already holds its live jobs (policy capacity.heavyQueued, 3, D8-CAP-2): nothing was written and the authorization stays ACTIVE; retry with the same key |

| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | SCHEMA_VIOLATION | REQUIRED, UNKNOWN_FIELD, TYPE, FORMAT, RANGE, ENUM | — | ADMISSION | the body does not satisfy the closed request schema (R2-058, R2-059); each field error names the member and why |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MISSING | — | — | ADMISSION | the Idempotency-Key header is absent |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID | — | — | ADMISSION | the Idempotency-Key header is not a key of the accepted shape |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | AMBIGUOUS_CREDENTIALS | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | a session cookie beside an Authorization header, or two session cookies: nothing is decided for either (B11 D-AUTH-5); the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | PATH_ID_INVALID | FORMAT | — | ADMISSION | a path parameter is not an id |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | AGENCY_SELECTOR_INVALID | — | — | ADMISSION | the X-Kauro-Agency selector is absent or not an id |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | — | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | no authenticated subject: the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | SUBJECT_UNAUTHENTICATED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the subject is not the identity the unit of work is bound to |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_DISABLED | — | — | ACCESS | the token's or the stored identity state is DISABLED |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED | — | — | ACCESS | the launch policy needs a verified email and enrolled MFA |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ORIGIN_REJECTED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request's Origin is not exactly an application origin (or absent), or the browser reports a cross-site request: nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | CSRF_TOKEN_INVALID | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request does not carry the session's CSRF token (X-Kauro-CSRF): nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | MEMBERSHIP_SUSPENDED | — | — | ACCESS | the caller's membership is SUSPENDED |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | MEMBERSHIP_NOT_ACTIVE | — | — | ACCESS | the caller's membership is not ACTIVE (INVITED) |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ROLE_INSUFFICIENT | — | — | ACCESS | the caller's role does not hold the route's role |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | TENANT_CONTEXT_MISSING | — | — | ACCESS | the request is not bound to the agency it selects |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | NOT_A_MEMBER | — | — | ACCESS | no live membership of the caller in the selected agency: a foreign and a nonexistent agency answer alike |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | — | — | — | COMMAND | the import stage is not visible to the caller's agency |
| 409 | IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT | REQUEST_DIGEST_DIFFERS | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the key was used with another request: nothing was executed again |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | OPERATION_PENDING | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the first request under this key is still in flight; retry with the same key |
| 409 | STALE_VERSION | — | — | always | COMMAND | expectedVersion is not the stage's current version, or validatedReceiptHash is not the digest of its latest validated receipt; the current safe version and state come back |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | STAGE_ALREADY_CONTINUED | — | always | COMMAND | the continuation was already recorded: it is recorded once; current names its version and state |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | STAGE_NOT_CONTINUABLE | — | always | COMMAND | the stage is not verified (or awaiting the approver's partial scope): it needs its receipt, a correction or an explicit recovery; current names its version and state |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | STAGE_EXPIRED | — | — | COMMAND | the stage's bounded workflow deadline passed on the database's clock: continuation needs explicit recovery, never guessed identities |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | PARENT_STAGE_NOT_VERIFIED | — | — | COMMAND | a required parent stage is not verified, or its workflow lapsed |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | CHILD_PLAN_NOT_APPROVED | — | — | COMMAND | the plan the stage belongs to is not an APPROVED plan |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | STAGE_TARGET_MISMATCH | — | — | COMMAND | the stage's target instance is not the project's current target: explicit recovery, not guessed IDs |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RECEIPT_DIGEST_MISMATCH | — | — | COMMAND | the stored receipt mapping is not the mapping its digest names |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |

| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | PLAN_NOT_VALIDATABLE | — | always | COMMAND | the plan is not VALIDATION_REQUIRED: only that state takes a validation intent; current names its version and state |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |

| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | SUBJECT_UNAUTHENTICATED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the subject is not the identity the unit of work is bound to |

| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED | — | — | ACCESS | the launch policy needs a verified email and enrolled MFA |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ORIGIN_REJECTED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request's Origin is not exactly an application origin (or absent), or the browser reports a cross-site request: nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |

| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | MEMBERSHIP_SUSPENDED | — | — | ACCESS | the caller's membership is SUSPENDED |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | MEMBERSHIP_NOT_ACTIVE | — | — | ACCESS | the caller's membership is not ACTIVE (INVITED) |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ROLE_INSUFFICIENT | — | — | ACCESS | the caller's role does not hold the route's role |
| 403 | ASSURANCE_REQUIRED | STEP_UP_MISSING | — | — | ASSURANCE | no step-up proof, or not a step-up proof |
| 403 | ASSURANCE_REQUIRED | STEP_UP_NOT_OWNED | — | — | ASSURANCE | the proof is not the caller's |
| 403 | ASSURANCE_REQUIRED | STEP_UP_STALE | — | — | ASSURANCE | the step-up is older than the freshness window or expired |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | TENANT_CONTEXT_MISSING | — | — | ACCESS | the request is not bound to the agency it selects |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | NOT_A_MEMBER | — | — | ACCESS | no live membership of the caller in the selected agency: a foreign and a nonexistent agency answer alike |
| 409 | IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT | REQUEST_DIGEST_DIFFERS | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the key was used with another request: nothing was executed again |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | OPERATION_PENDING | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the first request under this key is still in flight; retry with the same key |
| 409 | STALE_VERSION | — | — | always | COMMAND | expectedVersion is not the agency's current version; the current safe version and state come back |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | UNLISTED_TRANSITION | — | always | COMMAND | the agency is CLOSING or CLOSED: closure is requested from DRAFT, ACTIVE or RESTRICTED only |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | CLOSURE_OBLIGATIONS_UNRECONCILED | — | always | COMMAND | a payment order or refund of the agency is in flight or uncertain: it is settled or reconciled before the agency can close (open reconciliation cases do not refuse the request; they refuse the CLOSED edge) |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |

| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_DEPTH_EXCEEDED | — | — | ADMISSION | the body nests deeper than the parser bound |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_STRING_TOO_LONG | — | — | ADMISSION | a string of the body exceeds the parser bound |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | SCHEMA_VIOLATION | REQUIRED, UNKNOWN_FIELD, TYPE, FORMAT, RANGE, ENUM | — | ADMISSION | the body does not satisfy the closed request schema (R2-058, R2-059); each field error names the member and why |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MISSING | — | — | ADMISSION | the Idempotency-Key header is absent |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID | — | — | ADMISSION | the Idempotency-Key header is not a key of the accepted shape |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | AMBIGUOUS_CREDENTIALS | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | a session cookie beside an Authorization header, or two session cookies: nothing is decided for either (B11 D-AUTH-5); the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | — | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | no authenticated subject: the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | SUBJECT_UNAUTHENTICATED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the subject is not the identity the unit of work is bound to |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_DISABLED | — | — | ACCESS | the token's or the stored identity state is DISABLED |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED | — | — | ACCESS | the launch policy needs a verified email and enrolled MFA |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ORIGIN_REJECTED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request's Origin is not exactly an application origin (or absent), or the browser reports a cross-site request: nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | CSRF_TOKEN_INVALID | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request does not carry the session's CSRF token (X-Kauro-CSRF): nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | CORPORATE_ROLE_MISSING | — | — | ACCESS | the caller holds no ACTIVE KAURO_OWNER grant (read after its transition lock): an agency member, a seller and an unknown caller are refused alike |
| 403 | ASSURANCE_REQUIRED | STEP_UP_MISSING | — | — | ASSURANCE | no step-up proof, or not a step-up proof |
| 403 | ASSURANCE_REQUIRED | STEP_UP_NOT_OWNED | — | — | ASSURANCE | the proof is not the caller's |
| 403 | ASSURANCE_REQUIRED | STEP_UP_STALE | — | — | ASSURANCE | the step-up is older than the freshness window or expired |
| 404 | NOT_FOUND | — | — | — | COMMAND | the restore candidate is unknown |
| 409 | IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT | REQUEST_DIGEST_DIFFERS | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the key was used with another request: nothing was executed again |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | OPERATION_PENDING | — | — | IDEMPOTENCY | the first request under this key is still in flight; retry with the same key |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | RECOVERY_CANDIDATE_NOT_PREPARED | — | always | COMMAND | the candidate is already authorized: a restore authorizes once |
| 409 | PRECONDITION_FAILED | RECOVERY_DELETION_JOURNAL_MISMATCH | — | — | COMMAND | deletionJournalHash is not the candidate's own: a restore must not resurrect deleted rows |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | a stored fact the command relies on is not what its own records say; nonretryable until the subject or implementation changes |
| 422 | INTEGRITY_FAILURE | RESPONSE_CONTRACT_VIOLATION | — | — | PLATFORM | the reply its route's contract refused was not sent (R23, N13) |
| 500 | INTERNAL_ERROR | — | — | — | PLATFORM | an unexpected exception, recorded under the correlation id; retry with the same key |
| 503 | CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE | — | — | — | PLATFORM | no database capacity, a serialization or lock conflict, or a draining instance; retry with the same key |

## 4. Contract-test applicability (manual 10)

"For each command, tests cover the legitimate state, wrong role, wrong agency, stale version, duplicate request, changed-body
same key, invalid schema, lost response and relevant external uncertainty. Not every command needs every mutation if the risk
is absent; the catalogue records applicability and rationale." Each cited test is `apps/api/test/<file>` :: its title prefix;
the check proves every citation resolves to an existing test.

### CMD001 CreateAgency

| Mutation | Applicability |
| --- | --- |
| legitimate state | `cmd001-create-agency.test.ts` :: "OK: a verified identity creates one DRAFT agency…" |
| wrong role | `cmd001-create-agency.test.ts` :: "DENY: unverified identity → 403 NOT_AUTHORIZED IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED…"<br>`role-matrix.test.ts` :: "N17: every served command against every role…" |
| wrong agency | NOT_APPLICABLE: CreateAgency is identity-scoped (VERIFIED_USER, no agency selector); it creates the agency it acts on |
| stale version | NOT_APPLICABLE: a creation command carries no expectedVersion (registry fields: displayName) |
| duplicate request | `idempotency.test.ts` :: "same key + same body → 200/201 replayed:true with the original body…"<br>`idempotency.test.ts` :: "concurrent duplicate: two in-flight identical requests…" |
| changed-body same key | `idempotency.test.ts` :: "same key + different body → 409 IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT and no effect…" |
| invalid schema | `request-validation.test.ts` :: "manual-10 admission order…"<br>`schema-boundary.test.ts` :: "through the served routes, schema-shaped request members are refused…" |
| lost response | `idempotency.test.ts` :: "response lost after commit → retry with the same key returns the journaled result…" |
| external uncertainty | NOT_APPLICABLE: the command performs no external effect; every effect is rows of one database transaction, and an outbox row is dispatched later by its consumer's package (N20), whose uncertainty belongs to that consumer |

## 5. The manual-10 reads (re-check finding N16)

Every read runs under the caller's own context and policies after the same access decision as a command (identity, and for an
agency read an ACTIVE live membership after its transition lock; every membership role reads, AGENCY_READER); a denial is a
durable security fact under the read's action token. Lists page by keyset with an opaque cursor bound to the caller, the agency,
the list, its filter and its order (`api/cursor`, KEYSET_V1). What differs by role is the projection: a team manager
(MANAGE_TEAM) sees every membership with its lifecycle times, any other member the ACTIVE team's roles; every resource view
carries the caller's permitted actions (`api/permitted-actions`). Package B09's reads (D-REQ-B7) follow the same rules: the agency
reads its quote, order and refund under the agency selector (a resource outside its scope answers NOT_FOUND like a nonexistent one),
and the corporate owner reads a refund's review through its stored operation (STORED_OPERATION; KAURO_OWNER). Package B08's GetJob
(D8-API-1) is the status path of CMD036's 202: every member reads the job of the agency, a job of another agency answers NOT_FOUND,
and the view is the job's safe progress, never a provider identity, a fence or a lease; its GetAudit is the status path of CMD020's 202 under the
same rules (the view: phase, deadlines, grant classification and pending decisions, never a model task, a prompt or the pool).

| Read | Route | Scope | List | Reply view | Action token | Authority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ListAgencies | GET /api/v1/agencies | NONE | limit, cursor | AgencyPage | LIST_AGENCIES | 10 "Agency and team routes": "GET /agencies lists only actual memberships" |
| GetAgency | GET /api/v1/agencies/{agencyId} | AGENCY_PATH | — | AgencyView | GET_AGENCY | 10 "Agency and team routes": "GET /agencies/{agencyId} returns the safe agency view" |
| ListMembers | GET /api/v1/agencies/{agencyId}/members | AGENCY_PATH | limit, cursor | TeamPage | LIST_MEMBERS | 10 "Agency and team routes": "GET /agencies/{agencyId}/members returns only team information appropriate to the caller" |
| ListClients | GET /api/v1/agencies/{agencyId}/clients | AGENCY_PATH | limit, cursor | ClientPage | LIST_CLIENTS | 10 "Client and project routes": "GET lists safe client summaries with pagination" |

## 5a. The document intakes (B07 D-SEL-2; B08 D8-CMA-7)

A POST that stores a closed document once per content: 201 when stored, 200 with `created: false` when the same document was
already stored; no idempotency key and no journal entry (it changes no aggregate). The access decision is a read's (an ACTIVE live
membership after its transition lock) plus the intake's own role; every denial is a durable fact under its action token.

| Intake | Route | Scope | Reply view | Action token | Authority |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SubmitSelectionManifest | POST /api/v1/revisions/{revisionId}/selection-manifests | AGENCY_HEADER | SelectionManifestIntakeView | SUBMIT_SELECTION_MANIFEST | 10 "Upload and target routes" (selection edits require the revision version: the version is compared by CMD016); D-SEL-2 |
| SubmitDecisionArtifact | POST /api/v1/audits/{auditId}/decision-artifacts | AGENCY_HEADER | DecisionArtifactIntakeView | SUBMIT_DECISION_ARTIFACT | B08 design §8, §12 (D8-PLAN-1, D8-API-1): the agency's decisions enter as evidence, never through a command; B07 D-SEL-2: a non-journaled, content-idempotent intake |

| Status | Code | Reason | Field codes | current | Layer | Meaning |

| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | HEADER_REPEATED | — | — | TRANSPORT | a single-valued envelope header was sent more than once; nothing that depends on it was decided (R41) |

| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_SYNTAX | — | — | ADMISSION | the body is not strict UTF-8 JSON |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_DUPLICATE_KEY | — | — | ADMISSION | an object of the body repeats a member name |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_DEPTH_EXCEEDED | — | — | ADMISSION | the body nests deeper than the parser bound |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | JSON_STRING_TOO_LONG | — | — | ADMISSION | a string of the body exceeds the parser bound |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | SCHEMA_VIOLATION | REQUIRED, UNKNOWN_FIELD, TYPE, FORMAT, RANGE, ENUM | — | ADMISSION | the body does not satisfy the closed request schema (R2-058, R2-059); each field error names the member and why |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | AMBIGUOUS_CREDENTIALS | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | a session cookie beside an Authorization header, or two session cookies: nothing is decided for either (B11 D-AUTH-5); the anonymous denial is recorded |

| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | AGENCY_SELECTOR_INVALID | — | — | ADMISSION | the X-Kauro-Agency selector is absent or not an id |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | SELECTION_MANIFEST_MISMATCH | — | — | COMMAND | the document names another revision than the path |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | EXCLUSION_REASON_REQUIRED | — | — | COMMAND | an exclusion names no reason |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | HIDDEN_SHEET_NOT_ACKNOWLEDGED | — | — | COMMAND | a hidden worksheet is included without its explicit acknowledgement |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | PARSER_CHOICE_INVALID | — | — | COMMAND | an included table's parser choice does not fit its kind |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | PROJECTION_INVALID | — | — | COMMAND | a projection or association names what the document does not include |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | PROJECTION_KEY_NOT_CONFIRMED | — | — | COMMAND | a referenced-entity projection names no key the agency confirmed |
| 400 | INVALID_REQUEST | SELECTED_COLUMNS_LIMIT | — | — | COMMAND | a table selects more than 800 columns |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | — | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | no authenticated subject: the anonymous denial is recorded |
| 401 | AUTHENTICATION_REQUIRED | SUBJECT_UNAUTHENTICATED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the subject is not the identity the unit of work is bound to |

| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED | — | — | ACCESS | the launch policy needs a verified email and enrolled MFA |
| 403 | NOT_AUTHORIZED | ORIGIN_REJECTED | — | — | AUTHENTICATION | the request's Origin is not exactly an application origin (or absent), or the browser reports a cross-site request: nothing is executed and the key is not consumed |
